      })\n          .filter((zone) => zone.cellId && zone.containerId && zone.width !== 0);\n      } catch (_error) {\n        this.libraryCellDropZones = [];\n      }\n    },\n this.inspectorDirty = true;\n        }\n      } catch (_error) {\n        this.inspectorDirty = true;\n      } finally {\n        this.inspectorSaving = false;\n        if (this.inspectorSaveQueued)   //   wait for success, then close + refresh preview).\n    // Validation/network errors always keep the modal open and surface\n    // r3Error, regardless of which button triggered the save.\n    async saveActiveR3Settings({ closeOnSuccess = false } = {}) {\n      const modalBody = document.getElementById(\'sfbR3ModalBody\');\n      const form = modalBody &&  htmx.process(modalBody);\n        }\n        this.r3Error = \'ذخیره نشد؛ خطاهای فرم را بررسی کنید.\';\n        return false;\n      } catch (_error) {\n        this.r3Error = \'ذخیره انجام نشد. }\n    },\n    reloadPreview() {\n      const iframe = document.getElementById(\'sfbPreviewFrame\');\n      if (!iframe || !iframe.contentWindow) return;\n  if (direction === \'undo\' && !this.canUndo) return;\n      if (direction === \'redo\' && !this.canRedo) return;\n      this.historyBusy = true;\n      const endpoint = direction === \'undo\' ? true });\n      });\n    },\n    trapModalFocus(event) {\n      if (!this.$refs.addModal) return;\n      const focusable = [...this.$refs.addModal.querySelectorAll(\'button:not(:disabled), a[href], direction);\n        } else if (this.selectedContainerId) {\n          event.preventDefault();\n          this.containerCommand(this.selectedContainerId, direction);\n        }\n      }\n    },\n \'canvas\') { this.setMobilePanel(\'canvas\'); return; }\n      if (this.fullscreen) { this.fullscreen = false; return; }\n      if (this.selectedSectionId || this.selectedContainerId) productSectionForm(initialDataSource, initialProducts, maxItems = 60) {\n  var autoSource = initialDataSource && initialDataSource !== \'manual\' ? initialDataSource : \'newest\';\n  return {\n    dataSource: initialDataSource || \'newest\',\n    lastAutomaticSource: autoSource,\n    selectionMode: initialDataSource === \'manual\' ? \'manual\' : \'automatic\',\n    selectionQuery: \'\',\n !query || String(name || \'\').toLowerCase().includes(query);\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction itemRepeaterForm(initialRows, emptyRow) {\n  return {\n    rows: initialRows && initialRows.length ?
